# Agents · D · part 4

1,003 entries, data-researcher to database-architect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **data-researcher**: Researches EF Core, SQL, query optimization, transactions, and data-access topics for one dotnet-learning-agent run, using real web sources (Microsoft Learn first), and writes runs/&lt;run-id&gt;/research/data.md. Invoked only by the /prepare-interview coordinator with assigned topics and an output path.
